# Paper-to-Lean correspondence

For the current paper's section numbers and principal results, use the [current manuscript correspondence](current-paper-correspondence.md). The anchors and line numbers below refer to the preserved frozen manuscript.

This index is derived from the accepted S7 matrix, not a second claim ledger. It retains all 253 original IDs and their actual formal bindings. Definition/data entries encode the original model assumptions; their closure does not assert those assumptions for arbitrary inputs.
